out|-- decode_two_rules: rule scaffold generated by sta_mission_convert (LUA-15g).
out|-- Rules decoded from decode_two_rulesR.drl; actions, variable values and object handles
out|-- live in synthS.dsl and are left for the hand port. Regenerate, do not edit
out|-- the generated tables. Not in the .drl: the engine's rule scheduling. Here each
out|-- rule is a named condition monitor checked every tick that fires on a
out|-- false-to-true edge; the stock engine re-evaluates a rule when one of its
out|-- dependencies is marked dirty (that policy is not reproduced).
out|
out|local function T(v) return v ~= nil and v ~= false and v ~= 0 end
out|local function N(v) if v == true then return 1 elseif type(v) ~= 'number' then return 0 end return v end
out|
out|local Mission = {
out|    name = 'decode_two_rules',
out|    state = {},        -- the .dsl's rule variables, by name
out|    objects = {},      -- the .dsl's object handles, set by the ported setup
out|    timers = {},       -- running timer_int deadlines, by name
out|    rules = {},        -- the decoded rule table, in creation order
out|    ruleMonitors = {},
out|    missing = {},      -- engine calls the runtime does not bind yet: name -> count
out|    failed = {},       -- engine calls that raised an error: name -> count
out|    callFailed = false, -- set while a condition's engine call is unbound or failed
out|    time = 0,
out|}
out|
out|-- Engine calls of the conditions go to the A2 table by the ScriptInterface
out|-- method's own name. Unbound names and errors are counted, not raised, so an
out|-- unported mission still runs; a rule whose engine call gave no answer does
out|-- not hold that tick.
out|local function A(name, ...)
out|    local f = A2[name]
out|    if type(f) ~= 'function' then
out|        Mission.missing[name] = (Mission.missing[name] or 0) + 1
out|        Mission.callFailed = true
out|        return nil
out|    end
out|    local ok, v = pcall(f, ...)
out|    if not ok then
out|        Mission.failed[name] = (Mission.failed[name] or 0) + 1
out|        Mission.callFailed = true
out|        return nil
out|    end
out|    return v
out|end
out|
out|-- Variables (5): kind, and whether the engine supplies it.
out|Mission.variables = {
out|    { name = 'p_ALPHA', kind = 'hot_int' },
out|    { name = 'p_ALWAYS_EVALUATE', kind = 'dirty_int', engine = true },
out|    { name = 'p_POWER', kind = 'hot_float' },
out|    { name = 'p_ship', kind = 'hot_int' },
out|    { name = 'p_tWait', kind = 'timer_int' },
out|}
out|Mission.objectNames = { 'arr', 'ship' }
out|
out|-- Start values: the .dsl's setup assigns them; the hand port puts them here
out|-- (unlisted variables start at 0). The engine's ALWAYS_EVALUATE reads -1
out|-- (measured).
out|Mission.initial = {
out|    p_ALWAYS_EVALUATE = -1,
out|}
out|
out|-- Rules (2), in the order the .drl creates them.
out|Mission.rules[1] = {
out|    name = 'RuleOne',
out|    action = 'Do_Alpha',
out|    deps = { 'p_ALPHA', 'p_ALWAYS_EVALUATE' },
out|    when = function(S, O) return T(S.p_ALPHA) end,
out|}
out|Mission.rules[2] = {
out|    name = 'RuleTwo',
out|    action = 'Do_Beta',
out|    deps = { 'p_tWait' },
out|    when = function(S, O) return (2.0 * S.p_POWER) < 2.5 end,
out|}
out|
out|-- Actions (2): the .dsl functions the rules call. Port each body
out|-- from synthS.dsl; until then it only records that it ran.
out|Mission.ran = {}
out|function Mission:Do_Alpha() self.ran[#self.ran + 1] = 'Do_Alpha' end
out|function Mission:Do_Beta() self.ran[#self.ran + 1] = 'Do_Beta' end
out|
out|-- timer_int: reads 1 once its time has run out (measured); here starting or
out|-- stopping a timer clears it.
out|function Mission:startTimer(name, seconds)
out|    self.timers[name] = self.time + seconds
out|    self.state[name] = 0
out|end
out|
out|function Mission:stopTimer(name)
out|    self.timers[name] = nil
out|    self.state[name] = 0
out|end
out|
out|function Mission:setup()
out|    for _, v in ipairs(self.variables) do
out|        if self.state[v.name] == nil then self.state[v.name] = self.initial[v.name] or 0 end
out|    end
out|    for i, rule in ipairs(self.rules) do
out|        local monitor = Monitor(rule.name, false, nil, function()
out|            self.callFailed = false
out|            local holds = rule.when(self.state, self.objects) == true
out|            return holds and not self.callFailed  -- no answer from the engine: does not hold
out|        end, nil)
out|        monitor:hook(rule.name, MonitorHook.Type.OnTrue, nil, function()
out|            self[rule.action](self)
out|        end, nil)
out|        self.ruleMonitors[i] = monitor
out|    end
out|end
out|
out|function Mission:update(time)
out|    self.time = time
out|    for _, v in ipairs(self.variables) do
out|        local at = self.timers[v.name]
out|        if at and time > at then
out|            self.timers[v.name] = nil
out|            self.state[v.name] = 1
out|        end
out|    end
out|end
out|
out|MMM.register(Mission)

out|-- decode_two_rules_named: rule scaffold generated by sta_mission_convert (LUA-15g).
out|-- Rules decoded from decode_two_rules_namedR.drl; actions, variable values and object handles
out|-- live in synthS.dsl and are left for the hand port. Regenerate, do not edit
out|-- the generated tables. Not in the .drl: the engine's rule scheduling. Here each
out|-- rule is a named condition monitor checked every tick that fires on a
out|-- false-to-true edge; the stock engine re-evaluates a rule when one of its
out|-- dependencies is marked dirty (that policy is not reproduced).
out|
out|local function T(v) return v ~= nil and v ~= false and v ~= 0 end
out|local function N(v) if v == true then return 1 elseif type(v) ~= 'number' then return 0 end return v end
out|
out|local Mission = {
out|    name = 'decode_two_rules_named',
out|    state = {},        -- the .dsl's rule variables, by name
out|    objects = {},      -- the .dsl's object handles, set by the ported setup
out|    timers = {},       -- running timer_int deadlines, by name
out|    rules = {},        -- the decoded rule table, in creation order
out|    ruleMonitors = {},
out|    missing = {},      -- engine calls the runtime does not bind yet: name -> count
out|    failed = {},       -- engine calls that raised an error: name -> count
out|    callFailed = false, -- set while a condition's engine call is unbound or failed
out|    time = 0,
out|}
out|
out|-- Engine calls of the conditions go to the A2 table by the ScriptInterface
out|-- method's own name. Unbound names and errors are counted, not raised, so an
out|-- unported mission still runs; a rule whose engine call gave no answer does
out|-- not hold that tick.
out|local function A(name, ...)
out|    local f = A2[name]
out|    if type(f) ~= 'function' then
out|        Mission.missing[name] = (Mission.missing[name] or 0) + 1
out|        Mission.callFailed = true
out|        return nil
out|    end
out|    local ok, v = pcall(f, ...)
out|    if not ok then
out|        Mission.failed[name] = (Mission.failed[name] or 0) + 1
out|        Mission.callFailed = true
out|        return nil
out|    end
out|    return v
out|end
out|
out|-- Variables (5): kind, and whether the engine supplies it.
out|Mission.variables = {
out|    { name = 'p_ALPHA', kind = 'hot_int' },
out|    { name = 'p_ALWAYS_EVALUATE', kind = 'dirty_int', engine = true },
out|    { name = 'p_POWER', kind = 'hot_float' },
out|    { name = 'p_ship', kind = 'hot_int', engine = true },
out|    { name = 'p_tWait', kind = 'timer_int' },
out|}
out|Mission.objectNames = { 'arr', 'ship' }
out|
out|-- Start values: the .dsl's setup assigns them; the hand port puts them here
out|-- (unlisted variables start at 0). The engine's ALWAYS_EVALUATE reads -1
out|-- (measured).
out|Mission.initial = {
out|    p_ALWAYS_EVALUATE = -1,
out|}
out|
out|-- Rules (2), in the order the .drl creates them.
out|Mission.rules[1] = {
out|    name = 'RuleOne',
out|    action = 'Do_Alpha',
out|    deps = { 'p_ALPHA', 'p_ALWAYS_EVALUATE' },
out|    when = function(S, O) return T(S.p_ALPHA) end,
out|}
out|Mission.rules[2] = {
out|    name = 'RuleTwo',
out|    action = 'Do_Beta',
out|    deps = { 'p_tWait' },
out|    when = function(S, O) return (2.0 * S.p_POWER) < 2.5 end,
out|}
out|
out|-- Actions (2): the .dsl functions the rules call. Port each body
out|-- from synthS.dsl; until then it only records that it ran.
out|Mission.ran = {}
out|function Mission:Do_Alpha() self.ran[#self.ran + 1] = 'Do_Alpha' end
out|function Mission:Do_Beta() self.ran[#self.ran + 1] = 'Do_Beta' end
out|
out|-- timer_int: reads 1 once its time has run out (measured); here starting or
out|-- stopping a timer clears it.
out|function Mission:startTimer(name, seconds)
out|    self.timers[name] = self.time + seconds
out|    self.state[name] = 0
out|end
out|
out|function Mission:stopTimer(name)
out|    self.timers[name] = nil
out|    self.state[name] = 0
out|end
out|
out|function Mission:setup()
out|    for _, v in ipairs(self.variables) do
out|        if self.state[v.name] == nil then self.state[v.name] = self.initial[v.name] or 0 end
out|    end
out|    for i, rule in ipairs(self.rules) do
out|        local monitor = Monitor(rule.name, false, nil, function()
out|            self.callFailed = false
out|            local holds = rule.when(self.state, self.objects) == true
out|            return holds and not self.callFailed  -- no answer from the engine: does not hold
out|        end, nil)
out|        monitor:hook(rule.name, MonitorHook.Type.OnTrue, nil, function()
out|            self[rule.action](self)
out|        end, nil)
out|        self.ruleMonitors[i] = monitor
out|    end
out|end
out|
out|function Mission:update(time)
out|    self.time = time
out|    for _, v in ipairs(self.variables) do
out|        local at = self.timers[v.name]
out|        if at and time > at then
out|            self.timers[v.name] = nil
out|            self.state[v.name] = 1
out|        end
out|    end
out|end
out|
out|MMM.register(Mission)

out|-- decode_duplicate_rule_names: rule scaffold generated by sta_mission_convert (LUA-15g).
out|-- Rules decoded from decode_duplicate_rule_namesR.drl; actions, variable values and object handles
out|-- live in synthS.dsl and are left for the hand port. Regenerate, do not edit
out|-- the generated tables. Not in the .drl: the engine's rule scheduling. Here each
out|-- rule is a named condition monitor checked every tick that fires on a
out|-- false-to-true edge; the stock engine re-evaluates a rule when one of its
out|-- dependencies is marked dirty (that policy is not reproduced).
out|
out|local function T(v) return v ~= nil and v ~= false and v ~= 0 end
out|local function N(v) if v == true then return 1 elseif type(v) ~= 'number' then return 0 end return v end
out|
out|local Mission = {
out|    name = 'decode_duplicate_rule_names',
out|    state = {},        -- the .dsl's rule variables, by name
out|    objects = {},      -- the .dsl's object handles, set by the ported setup
out|    timers = {},       -- running timer_int deadlines, by name
out|    rules = {},        -- the decoded rule table, in creation order
out|    ruleMonitors = {},
out|    missing = {},      -- engine calls the runtime does not bind yet: name -> count
out|    failed = {},       -- engine calls that raised an error: name -> count
out|    callFailed = false, -- set while a condition's engine call is unbound or failed
out|    time = 0,
out|}
out|
out|-- Engine calls of the conditions go to the A2 table by the ScriptInterface
out|-- method's own name. Unbound names and errors are counted, not raised, so an
out|-- unported mission still runs; a rule whose engine call gave no answer does
out|-- not hold that tick.
out|local function A(name, ...)
out|    local f = A2[name]
out|    if type(f) ~= 'function' then
out|        Mission.missing[name] = (Mission.missing[name] or 0) + 1
out|        Mission.callFailed = true
out|        return nil
out|    end
out|    local ok, v = pcall(f, ...)
out|    if not ok then
out|        Mission.failed[name] = (Mission.failed[name] or 0) + 1
out|        Mission.callFailed = true
out|        return nil
out|    end
out|    return v
out|end
out|
out|-- Variables (5): kind, and whether the engine supplies it.
out|Mission.variables = {
out|    { name = 'p_ALPHA', kind = 'hot_int' },
out|    { name = 'p_ALWAYS_EVALUATE', kind = 'dirty_int', engine = true },
out|    { name = 'p_POWER', kind = 'hot_float' },
out|    { name = 'p_ship', kind = 'hot_int' },
out|    { name = 'p_tWait', kind = 'timer_int' },
out|}
out|Mission.objectNames = { 'arr', 'ship' }
out|
out|-- Start values: the .dsl's setup assigns them; the hand port puts them here
out|-- (unlisted variables start at 0). The engine's ALWAYS_EVALUATE reads -1
out|-- (measured).
out|Mission.initial = {
out|    p_ALWAYS_EVALUATE = -1,
out|}
out|
out|-- Rules (2), in the order the .drl creates them.
out|Mission.rules[1] = {
out|    name = 'RuleOne',
out|    action = 'Do_Alpha',
out|    deps = { 'p_ALPHA' },
out|    when = function(S, O) return T(S.p_ALPHA) end,
out|}
out|Mission.rules[2] = {
out|    name = 'RuleOne#2',
out|    action = 'Do_Beta',
out|    deps = { 'p_ALPHA' },
out|    when = function(S, O) return (2.0 * S.p_POWER) < 2.5 end,
out|}
out|
out|-- Actions (2): the .dsl functions the rules call. Port each body
out|-- from synthS.dsl; until then it only records that it ran.
out|Mission.ran = {}
out|function Mission:Do_Alpha() self.ran[#self.ran + 1] = 'Do_Alpha' end
out|function Mission:Do_Beta() self.ran[#self.ran + 1] = 'Do_Beta' end
out|
out|-- timer_int: reads 1 once its time has run out (measured); here starting or
out|-- stopping a timer clears it.
out|function Mission:startTimer(name, seconds)
out|    self.timers[name] = self.time + seconds
out|    self.state[name] = 0
out|end
out|
out|function Mission:stopTimer(name)
out|    self.timers[name] = nil
out|    self.state[name] = 0
out|end
out|
out|function Mission:setup()
out|    for _, v in ipairs(self.variables) do
out|        if self.state[v.name] == nil then self.state[v.name] = self.initial[v.name] or 0 end
out|    end
out|    for i, rule in ipairs(self.rules) do
out|        local monitor = Monitor(rule.name, false, nil, function()
out|            self.callFailed = false
out|            local holds = rule.when(self.state, self.objects) == true
out|            return holds and not self.callFailed  -- no answer from the engine: does not hold
out|        end, nil)
out|        monitor:hook(rule.name, MonitorHook.Type.OnTrue, nil, function()
out|            self[rule.action](self)
out|        end, nil)
out|        self.ruleMonitors[i] = monitor
out|    end
out|end
out|
out|function Mission:update(time)
out|    self.time = time
out|    for _, v in ipairs(self.variables) do
out|        local at = self.timers[v.name]
out|        if at and time > at then
out|            self.timers[v.name] = nil
out|            self.state[v.name] = 1
out|        end
out|    end
out|end
out|
out|MMM.register(Mission)

out|-- decode_condition_not_decoded: rule scaffold generated by sta_mission_convert (LUA-15g).
out|-- Rules decoded from decode_condition_not_decodedR.drl; actions, variable values and object handles
out|-- live in synthS.dsl and are left for the hand port. Regenerate, do not edit
out|-- the generated tables. Not in the .drl: the engine's rule scheduling. Here each
out|-- rule is a named condition monitor checked every tick that fires on a
out|-- false-to-true edge; the stock engine re-evaluates a rule when one of its
out|-- dependencies is marked dirty (that policy is not reproduced).
out|
out|local function T(v) return v ~= nil and v ~= false and v ~= 0 end
out|local function N(v) if v == true then return 1 elseif type(v) ~= 'number' then return 0 end return v end
out|
out|local Mission = {
out|    name = 'decode_condition_not_decoded',
out|    state = {},        -- the .dsl's rule variables, by name
out|    objects = {},      -- the .dsl's object handles, set by the ported setup
out|    timers = {},       -- running timer_int deadlines, by name
out|    rules = {},        -- the decoded rule table, in creation order
out|    ruleMonitors = {},
out|    missing = {},      -- engine calls the runtime does not bind yet: name -> count
out|    failed = {},       -- engine calls that raised an error: name -> count
out|    callFailed = false, -- set while a condition's engine call is unbound or failed
out|    time = 0,
out|}
out|
out|-- Engine calls of the conditions go to the A2 table by the ScriptInterface
out|-- method's own name. Unbound names and errors are counted, not raised, so an
out|-- unported mission still runs; a rule whose engine call gave no answer does
out|-- not hold that tick.
out|local function A(name, ...)
out|    local f = A2[name]
out|    if type(f) ~= 'function' then
out|        Mission.missing[name] = (Mission.missing[name] or 0) + 1
out|        Mission.callFailed = true
out|        return nil
out|    end
out|    local ok, v = pcall(f, ...)
out|    if not ok then
out|        Mission.failed[name] = (Mission.failed[name] or 0) + 1
out|        Mission.callFailed = true
out|        return nil
out|    end
out|    return v
out|end
out|
out|-- Variables (5): kind, and whether the engine supplies it.
out|Mission.variables = {
out|    { name = 'p_ALPHA', kind = 'hot_int' },
out|    { name = 'p_ALWAYS_EVALUATE', kind = 'dirty_int', engine = true },
out|    { name = 'p_POWER', kind = 'hot_float' },
out|    { name = 'p_ship', kind = 'hot_int' },
out|    { name = 'p_tWait', kind = 'timer_int' },
out|}
out|Mission.objectNames = { 'arr', 'ship' }
out|
out|-- Start values: the .dsl's setup assigns them; the hand port puts them here
out|-- (unlisted variables start at 0). The engine's ALWAYS_EVALUATE reads -1
out|-- (measured).
out|Mission.initial = {
out|    p_ALWAYS_EVALUATE = -1,
out|}
out|
out|-- Rules (2), in the order the .drl creates them.
out|Mission.rules[1] = {
out|    name = 'RuleOne',
out|    action = 'Do_Alpha',
out|    deps = { 'p_ALPHA', 'p_ALWAYS_EVALUATE' },
out|    -- condition not decoded: unsupported instruction `rol eax,0x3` at 0x1000404c
out|    when = function(S, O) return false end,
out|}
out|Mission.rules[2] = {
out|    name = 'RuleTwo',
out|    action = 'Do_Beta',
out|    deps = { 'p_tWait' },
out|    when = function(S, O) return (2.0 * S.p_POWER) < 2.5 end,
out|}
out|
out|-- Actions (2): the .dsl functions the rules call. Port each body
out|-- from synthS.dsl; until then it only records that it ran.
out|Mission.ran = {}
out|function Mission:Do_Alpha() self.ran[#self.ran + 1] = 'Do_Alpha' end
out|function Mission:Do_Beta() self.ran[#self.ran + 1] = 'Do_Beta' end
out|
out|-- timer_int: reads 1 once its time has run out (measured); here starting or
out|-- stopping a timer clears it.
out|function Mission:startTimer(name, seconds)
out|    self.timers[name] = self.time + seconds
out|    self.state[name] = 0
out|end
out|
out|function Mission:stopTimer(name)
out|    self.timers[name] = nil
out|    self.state[name] = 0
out|end
out|
out|function Mission:setup()
out|    for _, v in ipairs(self.variables) do
out|        if self.state[v.name] == nil then self.state[v.name] = self.initial[v.name] or 0 end
out|    end
out|    for i, rule in ipairs(self.rules) do
out|        local monitor = Monitor(rule.name, false, nil, function()
out|            self.callFailed = false
out|            local holds = rule.when(self.state, self.objects) == true
out|            return holds and not self.callFailed  -- no answer from the engine: does not hold
out|        end, nil)
out|        monitor:hook(rule.name, MonitorHook.Type.OnTrue, nil, function()
out|            self[rule.action](self)
out|        end, nil)
out|        self.ruleMonitors[i] = monitor
out|    end
out|end
out|
out|function Mission:update(time)
out|    self.time = time
out|    for _, v in ipairs(self.variables) do
out|        local at = self.timers[v.name]
out|        if at and time > at then
out|            self.timers[v.name] = nil
out|            self.state[v.name] = 1
out|        end
out|    end
out|end
out|
out|MMM.register(Mission)

out|-- decode_engine_getters: rule scaffold generated by sta_mission_convert (LUA-15g).
out|-- Rules decoded from decode_engine_gettersR.drl; actions, variable values and object handles
out|-- live in synthS.dsl and are left for the hand port. Regenerate, do not edit
out|-- the generated tables. Not in the .drl: the engine's rule scheduling. Here each
out|-- rule is a named condition monitor checked every tick that fires on a
out|-- false-to-true edge; the stock engine re-evaluates a rule when one of its
out|-- dependencies is marked dirty (that policy is not reproduced).
out|
out|local function T(v) return v ~= nil and v ~= false and v ~= 0 end
out|local function N(v) if v == true then return 1 elseif type(v) ~= 'number' then return 0 end return v end
out|
out|local Mission = {
out|    name = 'decode_engine_getters',
out|    state = {},        -- the .dsl's rule variables, by name
out|    objects = {},      -- the .dsl's object handles, set by the ported setup
out|    timers = {},       -- running timer_int deadlines, by name
out|    rules = {},        -- the decoded rule table, in creation order
out|    ruleMonitors = {},
out|    missing = {},      -- engine calls the runtime does not bind yet: name -> count
out|    failed = {},       -- engine calls that raised an error: name -> count
out|    callFailed = false, -- set while a condition's engine call is unbound or failed
out|    time = 0,
out|}
out|
out|-- Engine calls of the conditions go to the A2 table by the ScriptInterface
out|-- method's own name. Unbound names and errors are counted, not raised, so an
out|-- unported mission still runs; a rule whose engine call gave no answer does
out|-- not hold that tick.
out|local function A(name, ...)
out|    local f = A2[name]
out|    if type(f) ~= 'function' then
out|        Mission.missing[name] = (Mission.missing[name] or 0) + 1
out|        Mission.callFailed = true
out|        return nil
out|    end
out|    local ok, v = pcall(f, ...)
out|    if not ok then
out|        Mission.failed[name] = (Mission.failed[name] or 0) + 1
out|        Mission.callFailed = true
out|        return nil
out|    end
out|    return v
out|end
out|
out|-- Variables (5): kind, and whether the engine supplies it.
out|Mission.variables = {
out|    { name = 'p_ALPHA', kind = 'hot_int' },
out|    { name = 'p_ALWAYS_EVALUATE', kind = 'dirty_int', engine = true },
out|    { name = 'p_POWER', kind = 'hot_float' },
out|    { name = 'p_ship', kind = 'hot_int', engine = true },
out|    { name = 'p_tWait', kind = 'timer_int' },
out|}
out|Mission.objectNames = { 'arr', 'ship' }
out|
out|-- Start values: the .dsl's setup assigns them; the hand port puts them here
out|-- (unlisted variables start at 0). The engine's ALWAYS_EVALUATE reads -1
out|-- (measured).
out|Mission.initial = {
out|    p_ALWAYS_EVALUATE = -1,
out|}
out|
out|-- Rules (2), in the order the .drl creates them.
out|Mission.rules[1] = {
out|    name = 'RuleOne',
out|    action = 'Do_Alpha',
out|    deps = { 'p_ALPHA', 'p_ALWAYS_EVALUATE' },
out|    when = function(S, O) return T(S.p_ALPHA) end,
out|}
out|Mission.rules[2] = {
out|    name = 'RuleTwo',
out|    action = 'Do_Beta',
out|    deps = { 'p_tWait' },
out|    when = function(S, O) return (2.0 * S.p_POWER) < 2.5 end,
out|}
out|
out|-- Actions (2): the .dsl functions the rules call. Port each body
out|-- from synthS.dsl; until then it only records that it ran.
out|Mission.ran = {}
out|function Mission:Do_Alpha() self.ran[#self.ran + 1] = 'Do_Alpha' end
out|function Mission:Do_Beta() self.ran[#self.ran + 1] = 'Do_Beta' end
out|
out|-- timer_int: reads 1 once its time has run out (measured); here starting or
out|-- stopping a timer clears it.
out|function Mission:startTimer(name, seconds)
out|    self.timers[name] = self.time + seconds
out|    self.state[name] = 0
out|end
out|
out|function Mission:stopTimer(name)
out|    self.timers[name] = nil
out|    self.state[name] = 0
out|end
out|
out|function Mission:setup()
out|    for _, v in ipairs(self.variables) do
out|        if self.state[v.name] == nil then self.state[v.name] = self.initial[v.name] or 0 end
out|    end
out|    for i, rule in ipairs(self.rules) do
out|        local monitor = Monitor(rule.name, false, nil, function()
out|            self.callFailed = false
out|            local holds = rule.when(self.state, self.objects) == true
out|            return holds and not self.callFailed  -- no answer from the engine: does not hold
out|        end, nil)
out|        monitor:hook(rule.name, MonitorHook.Type.OnTrue, nil, function()
out|            self[rule.action](self)
out|        end, nil)
out|        self.ruleMonitors[i] = monitor
out|    end
out|end
out|
out|function Mission:update(time)
out|    self.time = time
out|    for _, v in ipairs(self.variables) do
out|        local at = self.timers[v.name]
out|        if at and time > at then
out|            self.timers[v.name] = nil
out|            self.state[v.name] = 1
out|        end
out|    end
out|end
out|
out|MMM.register(Mission)

out|        Mission.failed[name] = (Mission.failed[name] or 0) + 1
out|        Mission.callFailed = true
out|        return nil
out|    end
out|    return v
out|end

out|-- Variables (6): kind, and whether the engine supplies it.
out|Mission.variables = {
out|    { name = 'p_ALPHA', kind = 'hot_int' },
out|    { name = 'p_ALWAYS_EVALUATE', kind = 'dirty_int', engine = true },
out|    { name = 'p_POWER', kind = 'hot_float' },
out|    { name = 'p_UNUSED', kind = 'hot_int' },
out|    { name = 'p_ship', kind = 'hot_int' },
out|    { name = 'p_tWait', kind = 'timer_int' },
out|}

out|
out|-- Start values: the .dsl's setup assigns them; the hand port puts them here
out|-- (unlisted variables start at 0). The engine's ALWAYS_EVALUATE reads -1
out|-- (measured).
out|Mission.initial = {
out|    p_ALWAYS_EVALUATE = -1,
out|}
out|
out|-- Rules (2), in the order the .drl creates them.
out|Mission.rules[1] = {
out|    name = 'RuleOne',
out|    action = 'Do_Alpha',
out|    deps = { 'p_ALPHA', 'p_ALWAYS_EVALUATE' },
out|    when = function(S, O) return T(S.p_ALPHA) end,
out|}
out|Mission.rules[2] = {
out|    name = 'RuleTwo',
out|    action = 'Do_Beta',
out|    deps = { 'p_tWait' },
out|    when = function(S, O) return (2.0 * S.p_POWER) < 2.5 end,
out|}
out|
out|-- Actions (2): the .dsl functions the rules call. Port each body
out|-- from synthS.dsl; until then it only records that it ran.
out|Mission.ran = {}
out|function Mission:Do_Alpha() self.ran[#self.ran + 1] = 'Do_Alpha' end
out|function Mission:Do_Beta() self.ran[#self.ran + 1] = 'Do_Beta' end

out|-- timer_int: reads 1 once its time has run out (measured); here starting or
out|-- stopping a timer clears it.
out|function Mission:startTimer(name, seconds)
out|    self.timers[name] = self.time + seconds
out|    self.state[name] = 0
out|end
out|
out|function Mission:stopTimer(name)
out|    self.timers[name] = nil
out|    self.state[name] = 0
out|end
out|
out|function Mission:setup()
out|    for _, v in ipairs(self.variables) do
out|        if self.state[v.name] == nil then self.state[v.name] = self.initial[v.name] or 0 end
out|    end
out|    for i, rule in ipairs(self.rules) do
out|        local monitor = Monitor(rule.name, false, nil, function()
out|            self.callFailed = false
out|            local holds = rule.when(self.state, self.objects) == true
out|            return holds and not self.callFailed  -- no answer from the engine: does not hold
out|        end, nil)
out|        monitor:hook(rule.name, MonitorHook.Type.OnTrue, nil, function()
out|            self[rule.action](self)
out|        end, nil)
out|        self.ruleMonitors[i] = monitor
out|    end
out|end
out|
out|function Mission:update(time)
out|    self.time = time
out|    for _, v in ipairs(self.variables) do
out|        local at = self.timers[v.name]
out|        if at and time > at then
out|            self.timers[v.name] = nil
out|            self.state[v.name] = 1
out|        end
out|    end
out|end

out|-- decode_mission_name_from_file: rule scaffold generated by sta_mission_convert (LUA-15g).
out|-- Rules decoded from decode_mission_name_from_fileR.drl; actions, variable values and object handles
out|-- live in other.dsl and are left for the hand port. Regenerate, do not edit
out|-- the generated tables. Not in the .drl: the engine's rule scheduling. Here each
out|-- rule is a named condition monitor checked every tick that fires on a
out|-- false-to-true edge; the stock engine re-evaluates a rule when one of its
out|-- dependencies is marked dirty (that policy is not reproduced).
out|
out|local function T(v) return v ~= nil and v ~= false and v ~= 0 end
out|local function N(v) if v == true then return 1 elseif type(v) ~= 'number' then return 0 end return v end
out|
out|local Mission = {
out|    name = 'decode_mission_name_from_file',
out|    state = {},        -- the .dsl's rule variables, by name
out|    objects = {},      -- the .dsl's object handles, set by the ported setup
out|    timers = {},       -- running timer_int deadlines, by name
out|    rules = {},        -- the decoded rule table, in creation order
out|    ruleMonitors = {},
out|    missing = {},      -- engine calls the runtime does not bind yet: name -> count
out|    failed = {},       -- engine calls that raised an error: name -> count
out|    callFailed = false, -- set while a condition's engine call is unbound or failed
out|    time = 0,
out|}
out|
out|-- Engine calls of the conditions go to the A2 table by the ScriptInterface
out|-- method's own name. Unbound names and errors are counted, not raised, so an
out|-- unported mission still runs; a rule whose engine call gave no answer does
out|-- not hold that tick.
out|local function A(name, ...)
out|    local f = A2[name]
out|    if type(f) ~= 'function' then
out|        Mission.missing[name] = (Mission.missing[name] or 0) + 1
out|        Mission.callFailed = true
out|        return nil
out|    end
out|    local ok, v = pcall(f, ...)
out|    if not ok then
out|        Mission.failed[name] = (Mission.failed[name] or 0) + 1
out|        Mission.callFailed = true
out|        return nil
out|    end
out|    return v
out|end
out|
out|-- Variables (5): kind, and whether the engine supplies it.
out|Mission.variables = {
out|    { name = 'p_ALPHA', kind = 'hot_int' },
out|    { name = 'p_ALWAYS_EVALUATE', kind = 'dirty_int', engine = true },
out|    { name = 'p_POWER', kind = 'hot_float' },
out|    { name = 'p_ship', kind = 'hot_int' },
out|    { name = 'p_tWait', kind = 'timer_int' },
out|}
out|Mission.objectNames = { 'arr', 'ship' }
out|
out|-- Start values: the .dsl's setup assigns them; the hand port puts them here
out|-- (unlisted variables start at 0). The engine's ALWAYS_EVALUATE reads -1
out|-- (measured).
out|Mission.initial = {
out|    p_ALWAYS_EVALUATE = -1,
out|}
out|
out|-- Rules (2), in the order the .drl creates them.
out|Mission.rules[1] = {
out|    name = 'RuleOne',
out|    action = 'Do_Alpha',
out|    deps = { 'p_ALPHA', 'p_ALWAYS_EVALUATE' },
out|    when = function(S, O) return T(S.p_ALPHA) end,
out|}
out|Mission.rules[2] = {
out|    name = 'RuleTwo',
out|    action = 'Do_Beta',
out|    deps = { 'p_tWait' },
out|    when = function(S, O) return (2.0 * S.p_POWER) < 2.5 end,
out|}
out|
out|-- Actions (2): the .dsl functions the rules call. Port each body
out|-- from other.dsl; until then it only records that it ran.
out|Mission.ran = {}
out|function Mission:Do_Alpha() self.ran[#self.ran + 1] = 'Do_Alpha' end
out|function Mission:Do_Beta() self.ran[#self.ran + 1] = 'Do_Beta' end
out|
out|-- timer_int: reads 1 once its time has run out (measured); here starting or
out|-- stopping a timer clears it.
out|function Mission:startTimer(name, seconds)
out|    self.timers[name] = self.time + seconds
out|    self.state[name] = 0
out|end
out|
out|function Mission:stopTimer(name)
out|    self.timers[name] = nil
out|    self.state[name] = 0
out|end
out|
out|function Mission:setup()
out|    for _, v in ipairs(self.variables) do
out|        if self.state[v.name] == nil then self.state[v.name] = self.initial[v.name] or 0 end
out|    end
out|    for i, rule in ipairs(self.rules) do
out|        local monitor = Monitor(rule.name, false, nil, function()
out|            self.callFailed = false
out|            local holds = rule.when(self.state, self.objects) == true
out|            return holds and not self.callFailed  -- no answer from the engine: does not hold
out|        end, nil)
out|        monitor:hook(rule.name, MonitorHook.Type.OnTrue, nil, function()
out|            self[rule.action](self)
out|        end, nil)
out|        self.ruleMonitors[i] = monitor
out|    end
out|end
out|
out|function Mission:update(time)
out|    self.time = time
out|    for _, v in ipairs(self.variables) do
out|        local at = self.timers[v.name]
out|        if at and time > at then
out|            self.timers[v.name] = nil
out|            self.state[v.name] = 1
out|        end
out|    end
out|end
out|

out|-- decode_no_dsl_import: rule scaffold generated by sta_mission_convert (LUA-15g).
out|-- Rules decoded from decode_no_dsl_importR.drl; actions, variable values and object handles
out|-- live in decode_no_dsl_importS.dsl and are left for the hand port. Regenerate, do not edit
out|-- the generated tables. Not in the .drl: the engine's rule scheduling. Here each
out|-- rule is a named condition monitor checked every tick that fires on a
out|-- false-to-true edge; the stock engine re-evaluates a rule when one of its
out|-- dependencies is marked dirty (that policy is not reproduced).
out|
out|local function T(v) return v ~= nil and v ~= false and v ~= 0 end
out|local function N(v) if v == true then return 1 elseif type(v) ~= 'number' then return 0 end return v end
out|
out|local Mission = {
out|    name = 'decode_no_dsl_import',
out|    state = {},        -- the .dsl's rule variables, by name
out|    objects = {},      -- the .dsl's object handles, set by the ported setup
out|    timers = {},       -- running timer_int deadlines, by name
out|    rules = {},        -- the decoded rule table, in creation order
out|    ruleMonitors = {},
out|    missing = {},      -- engine calls the runtime does not bind yet: name -> count
out|    failed = {},       -- engine calls that raised an error: name -> count
out|    callFailed = false, -- set while a condition's engine call is unbound or failed
out|    time = 0,
out|}
out|
out|-- Engine calls of the conditions go to the A2 table by the ScriptInterface
out|-- method's own name. Unbound names and errors are counted, not raised, so an
out|-- unported mission still runs; a rule whose engine call gave no answer does
out|-- not hold that tick.
out|local function A(name, ...)
out|    local f = A2[name]
out|    if type(f) ~= 'function' then
out|        Mission.missing[name] = (Mission.missing[name] or 0) + 1
out|        Mission.callFailed = true
out|        return nil
out|    end
out|    local ok, v = pcall(f, ...)
out|    if not ok then
out|        Mission.failed[name] = (Mission.failed[name] or 0) + 1
out|        Mission.callFailed = true
out|        return nil
out|    end
out|    return v
out|end
out|
out|-- Variables (5): kind, and whether the engine supplies it.
out|Mission.variables = {
out|    { name = 'p_ALPHA', kind = 'hot_int', engine = true },
out|    { name = 'p_ALWAYS_EVALUATE', kind = 'dirty_int', engine = true },
out|    { name = 'p_POWER', kind = 'hot_float', engine = true },
out|    { name = 'p_ship', kind = 'hot_int', engine = true },
out|    { name = 'p_tWait', kind = 'timer_int', engine = true },
out|}
out|Mission.objectNames = {  }
out|
out|-- Start values: the .dsl's setup assigns them; the hand port puts them here
out|-- (unlisted variables start at 0). The engine's ALWAYS_EVALUATE reads -1
out|-- (measured).
out|Mission.initial = {
out|    p_ALWAYS_EVALUATE = -1,
out|}
out|
out|-- Rules (2), in the order the .drl creates them.
out|Mission.rules[1] = {
out|    name = 'RuleOne',
out|    action = 'Do_Alpha',
out|    deps = { 'p_ALPHA', 'p_ALWAYS_EVALUATE' },
out|    when = function(S, O) return T(S.p_ALPHA) end,
out|}
out|Mission.rules[2] = {
out|    name = 'RuleTwo',
out|    action = 'Do_Beta',
out|    deps = { 'p_tWait' },
out|    when = function(S, O) return (2.0 * S.p_POWER) < 2.5 end,
out|}
out|
out|-- Actions (2): the .dsl functions the rules call. Port each body
out|-- from decode_no_dsl_importS.dsl; until then it only records that it ran.
out|Mission.ran = {}
out|function Mission:Do_Alpha() self.ran[#self.ran + 1] = 'Do_Alpha' end
out|function Mission:Do_Beta() self.ran[#self.ran + 1] = 'Do_Beta' end
out|
out|-- timer_int: reads 1 once its time has run out (measured); here starting or
out|-- stopping a timer clears it.
out|function Mission:startTimer(name, seconds)
out|    self.timers[name] = self.time + seconds
out|    self.state[name] = 0
out|end
out|
out|function Mission:stopTimer(name)
out|    self.timers[name] = nil
out|    self.state[name] = 0
out|end
out|
out|function Mission:setup()
out|    for _, v in ipairs(self.variables) do
out|        if self.state[v.name] == nil then self.state[v.name] = self.initial[v.name] or 0 end
out|    end
out|    for i, rule in ipairs(self.rules) do
out|        local monitor = Monitor(rule.name, false, nil, function()
out|            self.callFailed = false
out|            local holds = rule.when(self.state, self.objects) == true
out|            return holds and not self.callFailed  -- no answer from the engine: does not hold
out|        end, nil)
out|        monitor:hook(rule.name, MonitorHook.Type.OnTrue, nil, function()
out|            self[rule.action](self)
out|        end, nil)
out|        self.ruleMonitors[i] = monitor
out|    end
out|end
out|
out|function Mission:update(time)
out|    self.time = time
out|    for _, v in ipairs(self.variables) do
out|        local at = self.timers[v.name]
out|        if at and time > at then
out|            self.timers[v.name] = nil
out|            self.state[v.name] = 1
out|        end
out|    end
out|end
out|
out|MMM.register(Mission)
